users

-- ---------- users / auth ----------
CREATE TABLE users (
  id            uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  display_name  text        NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(display_name) BETWEEN 1 AND 50),
  email         text        UNIQUE CHECK (email = lower(email)),
  avatar_key    text,
  locale        text        NOT NULL DEFAULT 'zh-TW',
  is_staff      boolean     NOT NULL DEFAULT false,   -- 營運人員（MVP 起：/admin 後台；P2：人工代購）
  notification_prefs jsonb  NOT NULL DEFAULT '{"email_claims": true}'::jsonb,   -- 通知設定（PATCH /me）；鍵：email_claims、email_birthdays（缺鍵 = 開啟，D65）
  handle        text        CHECK (handle ~ '^[a-z0-9_]{3,30}$'),     -- v0.5：個人頁 /u/{handle}；NULL = 尚未設定（無個人頁）；UNIQUE（partial，見 users_handle_key）
  default_claim_visibility share_level NOT NULL DEFAULT 'private',    -- v0.5：登入者捐助 / 點數認捐的預設公開層級
  bio           text        CHECK (char_length(bio) BETWEEN 1 AND 160),  -- 0014：自我介紹，個人頁顯示
  birthday      text        CHECK (birthday ~ '^(0[1-9]|1[0-2])-(0[1-9]|[12][0-9]|3[01])$'),  -- 0014：'MM-DD'，不收年份
  birthday_visibility share_level NOT NULL DEFAULT 'friends',       -- 0014：個人頁誰看得到生日
  birthday_notified_year smallint,                          -- 0043（D65）：已寄過朋友生日通知的生日年份（條件式 UPDATE 保證每年一次）
  birthday_nudged_year   smallint,                          -- 0043：已提醒壽星開清單的生日年份
  onboarding_done_at     timestamptz,                       -- 0044（D66）：新手導覽完成或跳過的時間；NULL = 導覽進行中（既有帳號上線時記為已完成）
  blocked_at    timestamptz,                              -- 封鎖（拒付 / 盜刷購買者）
  anonymized_at timestamptz,                              -- 帳號刪除去識別化時間
  created_at    timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at    timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  deleted_at    timestamptz
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX users_handle_key ON users (handle) WHERE handle IS NOT NULL;   -- v0.5
欄位說明
idUUID v7 PK
display_name顯示名稱 1-50 字
email小寫 email；可為 NULL（僅 LINE 登入且未授權 email）
avatar_keyR2 object key
locale預設 zh-TW
deleted_at軟刪除（帳號刪除流程，P1）
is_staff營運人員（<strong>MVP 起</strong>：前端 /admin 後台、STAFF_ONLY 守衛；P2：人工代購 /admin/orders、/admin/cash-refunds、/admin/corporate）
notification_prefsjsonb，通知設定（PATCH /me 更新）；MVP 鍵 {"email_claims": true|false}（D47-5，不使用 notify_email）
handlev0.5：個人頁代號，^[a-z0-9_]{3,30}$，儲存時轉小寫；partial UNIQUE（users_handle_key ... WHERE handle IS NOT NULL）；NULL = 未設定，無個人頁；經 PATCH /me 設定，重複回 409 HANDLE_TAKEN
default_claim_visibilityv0.5：share_level，預設 private。登入者捐助或點數認捐未指定 visibility 時由 trigger 套用（寫入當下快照）；修改預設不回溯既有紀錄
bio0014：自我介紹 1–160 字，NULL = 未填；個人頁一律顯示
birthday / birthday_visibility0014：生日只存月日 MM-DD（個資最少蒐集，不收年份；日期合法性由 API 以閏年驗證，允許 02-29）；birthday_visibility 為 share_level，預設 friends，決定 GET /users/{handle} 是否回傳生日
blocked_at封鎖時間（拒付 / 盜刷購買者）；封鎖後不可儲值與購買禮物卡
anonymized_at帳號刪除去識別化時間（D40）：display_name 改為「已刪除的使用者」、email / avatar / bio / birthday 清空、identities 刪除；列本身保留以維持外鍵與金融紀錄假名化

auth_identities

CREATE TABLE auth_identities (
  id            uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  user_id       uuid          NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  provider      auth_provider NOT NULL,
  provider_uid  text          NOT NULL,           -- email: 小寫 email；line/google: sub
  email         text,
  created_at    timestamptz   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  UNIQUE (provider, provider_uid)
);
CREATE INDEX auth_identities_user_idx ON auth_identities (user_id);
欄位說明
provideremail | line | google（補充枚舉 auth_provider）
provider_uidemail 用小寫 email；line/google 用 OIDC sub
(provider, provider_uid)UNIQUE，同一外部身分只能綁一個 user

otp_challenges

-- 補充表：Email OTP 挑戰（規格未列，標註新增）
CREATE TABLE otp_challenges (
  id            uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  email         text        NOT NULL CHECK (email = lower(email)),
  code_hash     bytea       NOT NULL,             -- SHA-256(code || server_pepper)
  attempts      smallint    NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  expires_at    timestamptz NOT NULL,
  consumed_at   timestamptz,
  created_at    timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
CREATE INDEX otp_challenges_email_idx ON otp_challenges (email, created_at DESC);
欄位說明
（補充表）規格未列，Email OTP 需要；ws 內部使用，不對外暴露
code_hash6 位數驗證碼的 SHA-256（加 server pepper），不存明文
attempts錯誤次數，達 5 次作廢
expires_at建立後 10 分鐘
consumed_at驗證成功時間，防重放

sessions

CREATE TABLE sessions (
  id            uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  user_id       uuid        NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  token_hash    bytea       NOT NULL UNIQUE,      -- SHA-256(opaque token)
  user_agent    text,
  ip            inet,
  expires_at    timestamptz NOT NULL,
  last_seen_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  revoked_at    timestamptz,
  created_at    timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
CREATE INDEX sessions_user_idx ON sessions (user_id);
CREATE INDEX sessions_expires_idx ON sessions (expires_at) WHERE revoked_at IS NULL;
欄位說明
token_hashopaque session token（32 bytes）的 SHA-256；cookie ws_session 存明文 token
expires_at預設 30 天滑動過期
revoked_atlogout 時寫入

user_activity_days

-- 0015：每日活躍（一人一天一列，台北日）；只存日期，不存時間 / IP / 裝置
CREATE TABLE user_activity_days (
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  day     date NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id, day)
);
CREATE INDEX user_activity_days_day_idx ON user_activity_days (day);
欄位說明
dayAsia/Taipei 日期。唯一寫入點為 CurrentUser extractor：登入後第一個已驗證請求、跨日後第一個請求、其後每 5 分鐘最多一次，先於 sessions.last_seen_at 寫入（ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING；對 users 列用 FOR KEY SHARE SKIP LOCKED，避免與刪帳 / 停權的 users 鎖死結，被鎖時略過、下個請求補寫）；停權 / 撤銷的 session 不寫入
保存背景清理每小時刪除 day < 台北今天 − 400 的列（保留 400 天）；刪帳時同交易刪除該使用者全部列；不納入個資匯出（與 sessions 同為營運紀錄）。0015 由既有 sessions 的 created_at / last_seen_at 回填（未刪除使用者）

guests

CREATE TABLE guests (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  guest_token_hash bytea       NOT NULL UNIQUE,   -- SHA-256(32 bytes token)
  display_name     text        NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(display_name) BETWEEN 1 AND 30),
  contact          text        CHECK (char_length(contact) <= 100),
  email            text        CHECK (email = lower(email)),   -- 選填：捐助確認信與「管理我的捐助」連結（D39）
  deleted_at       timestamptz,                                  -- DELETE /guest/me：清除暱稱 / 聯絡方式 / email，claims 保留
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  last_seen_at     timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
欄位說明
D50（legacy）：不再建立新的 guests 列，本表（與 claims.guest_id、claims_single_actor、guest_recovery_tokens）僅保留供 D50 前的舊訪客認捐，無 migration。
guest_token_hashSHA-256(guest_token 32 bytes)，UNIQUE；明文僅曾在首次回應與 cookie 出現（D50 前）
display_name訪客暱稱 1-30 字
contact選填聯絡方式（LINE ID / Email / 電話），僅建立者在非驚喜鎖定期可見
last_seen_at每次帶 token 請求時更新（可節流）
email選填：捐助確認信與「管理我的捐助」連結（D39）；小寫
deleted_atDELETE /guest/me：暱稱改為「已刪除的訪客」，清除 contact / email；claims 保留

guest_recovery_tokens

-- 訪客恢復權杖（D47-3）：一次性、30 天有效；信件只放權杖，不放 guest token
CREATE TABLE guest_recovery_tokens (
  id          uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  guest_id    uuid        NOT NULL REFERENCES guests(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  token_hash  bytea       NOT NULL UNIQUE,           -- SHA-256(權杖)；權杖明文只出現在信件連結 /me/claims#r={token}
  expires_at  timestamptz NOT NULL,                  -- 建立後 30 天
  used_at     timestamptz,
  created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
CREATE INDEX guest_recovery_tokens_guest_idx ON guest_recovery_tokens (guest_id, created_at DESC);
欄位說明
token_hashSHA-256(恢復權杖)，UNIQUE；權杖明文只出現在信件連結 /me/claims#r={token}，信件不放 guest token（D47-3）
expires_at / used_at建立後 30 天；一次性——驗證成功即標 used_at；每次寄信產生新權杖
（換發）POST /guest/recover 成功時覆寫 guests.guest_token_hash，舊裝置的舊 token 同時失效；無效 / 過期 / 已用 / 訪客已刪除一律 404 NOT_FOUND
0041_orgs_relief（2026-10-27，PRD D60 至 D64）：本節以下的 organizations、org_members 與 relief 相關表 / 欄位以 db/migrations/0041_orgs_relief.sql 的實作為準，取代先前的 P2-D 草案（organizations.name / verified_by / verification_doc_keys、wishlists.location / address、receipts.claim_id / client_request_id / received_at）。PostGIS 與地圖查詢整段延後（D64）：沒有 location、GiST 索引、CREATE EXTENSION postgis 不在這一階段。捐款收據（開立收據給捐贈者）延到第二階段（D63），0041 的 receipts 表是「據點實收登記」，與收據無關。

organizations

CREATE TYPE org_role AS ENUM ('owner', 'coordinator', 'volunteer');

-- ---------- 組織 ----------
-- 草稿階段只有 legal_name / display_name 必填；送審（非 draft）後其餘欄位都要齊。
CREATE TABLE organizations (
  id                  uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  slug                char(10)    NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  legal_name          text        NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(legal_name) BETWEEN 1 AND 100),
  display_name        text        NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(display_name) BETWEEN 1 AND 50),
  org_type            text        CHECK (org_type IN ('association', 'foundation', 'religious', 'other')),
  tax_id              char(8)     CHECK (tax_id ~ '^[0-9]{8}$'),
  registration_no     text,
  authority           text,
  website             text,
  intro               text        CHECK (char_length(intro) <= 2000),
  logo_key            text,
  rep_name            text,
  contact_name        text,
  contact_title       text,
  contact_phone_enc   bytea,      -- sealed.rs 加密，不對外
  public_email        text,
  public_phone        text,
  verification_status text        NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft'
                      CHECK (verification_status IN ('draft', 'pending', 'needs_changes', 'verified', 'rejected')),
  review_note         text,
  flagged_fields      text[]      NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
  resubmit_after      timestamptz,
  submitted_at        timestamptz,
  verified_at         timestamptz,
  created_by          uuid        NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  created_at          timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at          timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  CONSTRAINT organizations_verified_at CHECK ((verification_status = 'verified') = (verified_at IS NOT NULL)),
  CONSTRAINT organizations_submit_required CHECK (verification_status = 'draft' OR (
    org_type IS NOT NULL AND tax_id IS NOT NULL AND registration_no IS NOT NULL AND authority IS NOT NULL
    AND rep_name IS NOT NULL AND contact_name IS NOT NULL AND contact_phone_enc IS NOT NULL AND public_email IS NOT NULL))
);
-- 同一統編同時只能有一筆「在審 / 已通過」；被拒的不佔位
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX organizations_tax_id_live ON organizations (tax_id)
  WHERE verification_status IN ('pending', 'needs_changes', 'verified');
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX organizations_one_draft ON organizations (created_by) WHERE verification_status = 'draft';
CREATE TRIGGER organizations_set_updated_at BEFORE UPDATE ON organizations FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at();

CREATE TABLE org_members (
  org_id      uuid        NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_id     uuid        NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  role        org_role    NOT NULL,
  handle      text        CHECK (char_length(handle) <= 30),
  disabled_at timestamptz,
  created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  PRIMARY KEY (org_id, user_id)
);
CREATE INDEX org_members_user_idx ON org_members (user_id);

CREATE TABLE org_invites (
  id          uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  org_id      uuid        NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  email       text        NOT NULL CHECK (email = lower(email)),
  role        org_role    NOT NULL,
  handle      text,
  token_hash  bytea       NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  invited_by  uuid        NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  expires_at  timestamptz NOT NULL,
  accepted_at timestamptz,
  cancelled_at timestamptz,
  created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE org_documents (
  id           uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  org_id       uuid        NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  kind         text        NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('registration', 'permit')),
  object_key   text        NOT NULL,
  content_type text        NOT NULL,
  size_bytes   int         NOT NULL,
  uploaded_by  uuid        NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  created_at   timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE org_permits (
  id          uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  org_id      uuid        NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  permit_no   text        NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(permit_no) BETWEEN 1 AND 60),
  valid_from  date        NOT NULL,
  valid_to    date        NOT NULL,
  document_id uuid        REFERENCES org_documents(id),
  status      text        NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending' CHECK (status IN ('pending', 'approved', 'rejected')),
  review_note text,
  created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  CHECK (valid_to >= valid_from)
);
欄位說明
verification_statusdraft | pending | needs_changes | verified | rejected。送審（非 draft）後 org_type、tax_id、registration_no、authority、rep_name、contact_name、contact_phone_enc、public_email 皆必填（CHECK）；verified 與 verified_at 一致。
tax_id8 碼，應用層驗證檢查碼（TAX_ID_CHECKSUM）；同一統編同時只能有一筆 pending / needs_changes / verified（部分唯一索引，違反回 TAX_ID_TAKEN），被拒的不佔位。
organizations_one_draft每位建立者同時只能有一份 draft（ORG_DRAFT_EXISTS）。
flagged_fields / review_noteneeds_changes 時審核者標記的欄位（含 documents、permits）與說明；組織方只能改被標記的欄位（FIELD_LOCKED）。
resubmit_after被拒（rejected）時設為 now() + 30 天；之前 PATCH 回 RESUBMIT_TOO_EARLY，之後 PATCH 會把狀態重置為 draft 重新申請。
contact_phone_encsealed.rs 加密（SHIPPING_ENC_KEY），只對 owner 與 staff 解密回傳為 contact_phone，不對外公開。
org_members(org_id, user_id) 複合 PK；role owner | coordinator | volunteer；disabled_at 非 NULL 為停用（帳號刪除、owner 停用時設定，重新接受邀請可恢復）。至少保留一位有效 owner（LAST_OWNER）。
org_invites以 email 邀請，token_hash 存 SHA-256；7 天效期；accepted_at / cancelled_at 標記終態。
org_documents立案文件（registration）與許可文件（permit），內容以 sealed.rs 加密後存私有儲存，不經 CDN；PDF / JPEG / PNG，每檔 10 MB 內。
org_permits勸募許可：permit_no、valid_from / valid_to（台北日期）、status pending | approved | rejected。據點上線須許可 approved 且今天在效期內。

relief_events

-- ---------- 救災事件 ----------
CREATE TABLE relief_events (
  id                uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  slug              char(10)    NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  name              text        NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(name) BETWEEN 1 AND 80),
  hazard_type       text        NOT NULL CHECK (hazard_type IN ('earthquake', 'typhoon', 'flood', 'fire', 'other')),
  counties          text[]      NOT NULL CHECK (cardinality(counties) >= 1),
  area_note         text        CHECK (char_length(area_note) <= 200),
  occurred_on       date,
  description       text        CHECK (char_length(description) <= 2000),
  created_by_org    uuid        NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  merged_into       uuid        REFERENCES relief_events(id),
  moderation_status moderation_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ok',
  moderation_reason text,
  created_at        timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at        timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
CREATE TRIGGER relief_events_set_updated_at BEFORE UPDATE ON relief_events FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at();
欄位說明
created_by_org建立事件的組織；事件屬公共資源，任何組織的據點都可掛上，只有建立組織可編輯名稱 / 說明 / 區域備註。
counties臺北市 等 22 縣市正式名稱（「臺」字），至少一個；前後端共用同一份固定順序清單。
merged_into平台營運合併重複事件；公開頁沿此鏈走到最終事件（最多 10 跳）。
moderation_statusok | hidden；下架會暫停事件下所有上線據點，取消下架不會自動恢復據點。
0041 對既有表的修訂：
  • wishlists 加 org_id、event_id、permit_id、site_address、site_status（active | paused | closed）。CHECK wishlists_relief_site：type = relief ⇔ 五欄全有，非 relief 全為 NULL；wishlists_relief_public：relief 一律 visibility = public。據點建立時 status = draft、site_status = active。
  • wishlist_items 加 category（water | food | medical | hygiene | shelter | clothing | baby | other）與 urgency（critical | high | medium | low）；trigger items_relief_shape：relief 品項兩者必填，其餘清單必為 NULL。兩種 funding_mode（quantity、crowdfund）皆可（D62，推翻舊版「relief 只能 quantity」）。
  • trigger wishlists_relief_publish：據點變成 active（或換許可 / 換組織）時，組織須 verified 且許可 approved 且在效期內，否則拋 check_violation；只在這些欄位實際改變時才檢查，已上線據點的暫停 / 關閉不受許可到期影響。這是安全網，應用層先回 ORG_NOT_VERIFIED / PERMIT_INVALID。
  • receipts：欄位 id、item_id、wishlist_id、qty（>0）、note（≤200）、recorded_by、created_at；實收 qty_received 為 SUM(receipts.qty)，由查詢計算，不另存欄位。
  • 函式 can_manage_wishlist(owner, org, uid)：個人清單看 owner_id；組織清單看 org_members 的 owner / coordinator（未停用）。清單擁有權檢查一律用它（SQL 以 org_id IS NULL AND owner_id = $uid OR can_manage_wishlist(...) 的形式），建立者離職後不再有權限。
  • 舊草案的 wishlists_check_org_verified、wishlists_relief_requires_site、wishlists_site_status_only_relief、wishlists_location_gist、wishlists_public_sites_idx 與 wishlist_items_validate 中的 quantity 限制不存在（下方 wishlists / wishlist_items DDL 中的相關片段為舊草案，以本框為準）。

wishlists

-- ---------- wishlists ----------
CREATE TABLE wishlists (
  id                 uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  owner_id           uuid            NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  org_id             uuid            REFERENCES organizations(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  type               wishlist_type   NOT NULL,
  status             wishlist_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft',
  visibility         visibility      NOT NULL DEFAULT 'friends',   -- D51：原預設 link；0016 起預設 friends
  slug               char(10)        NOT NULL,
  title              text            NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(title) BETWEEN 1 AND 100),
  description        text,
  cover_image_key    text,
  cover_image_status image_status    NOT NULL DEFAULT 'none',
  cover_preset       text            CHECK (cover_preset IN ('birthday','wedding','baby','housewarming','graduation','holiday','travel','gift')), -- 預設封面 id，圖在前端 /covers/{id}.svg
  event_date         date,
  birthday_notify    boolean         NOT NULL DEFAULT false,   -- 0043（D65）：生日清單通知；每位 owner 最多一份（partial unique）；POST/PATCH 可帶；清單改 private 自動關
  show_claimer_names boolean         NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  surprise_mode      boolean         NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  location           geography(Point, 4326),
  address            text,
  site_status        site_status,
  moderation_status  moderation_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ok',   -- hidden → 公開頁 410 WISHLIST_REMOVED
  moderation_reason  text,
  moderated_at       timestamptz,
  moderated_by       uuid            REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  claim_ttl_hours    integer         CHECK (claim_ttl_hours > 0),   -- 購買期限（小時）；NULL = 不限；personal / registry 新建預設 168（Q12，0030 也把既有 NULL 補成 168）；relief 預設 48
  closes_at          timestamptz,                                   -- NULL = 不設期限；到期由背景 job 將 active 轉 closed（0009）
  closes_reminded_at timestamptz,                                   -- 到期前 24 小時提醒已寄出的時間；改 closes_at 時清空（0010）
  closed_at          timestamptz,
  created_at         timestamptz     NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at         timestamptz     NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  deleted_at         timestamptz,
  CONSTRAINT wishlists_slug_format CHECK (slug ~ '^[0-9A-Za-z]{10}$'),
  -- [舊草案，未實作；見 0041 修訂框：wishlists_relief_site / wishlists_relief_public]
  CONSTRAINT wishlists_relief_requires_site CHECK (
    type <> 'relief' OR (org_id IS NOT NULL AND location IS NOT NULL AND site_status IS NOT NULL)),
  CONSTRAINT wishlists_site_status_only_relief CHECK (type = 'relief' OR site_status IS NULL),
  CONSTRAINT wishlists_surprise_not_relief CHECK (NOT surprise_mode OR type <> 'relief'),
  -- v0.5：wishlists_public_only_relief 已移除（0005_social）：personal / registry 也可 public
  CONSTRAINT wishlists_visibility_supported CHECK (visibility::text NOT IN ('link', 'password')),   -- D51（0016）；原 wishlists_password_has_hash 與 access_password_hash 欄位已移除
  CONSTRAINT wishlists_hidden_has_reason CHECK (moderation_status = 'ok' OR moderation_reason IS NOT NULL),
  CONSTRAINT wishlists_relief_no_names CHECK (type <> 'relief' OR show_claimer_names = false)
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX wishlists_slug_key ON wishlists (slug);
CREATE INDEX wishlists_owner_idx ON wishlists (owner_id, status) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX wishlists_hidden_idx ON wishlists (moderated_at DESC) WHERE moderation_status = 'hidden';
CREATE INDEX wishlists_org_idx ON wishlists (org_id) WHERE org_id IS NOT NULL;
-- [延後，D64] CREATE INDEX wishlists_location_gist ON wishlists USING gist (location) WHERE type = 'relief';  -- PostGIS 地圖，第一段不做
-- [延後，D64] 地圖列表熱路徑：公開、進行中的據點
CREATE INDEX wishlists_public_sites_idx ON wishlists (updated_at DESC)
  WHERE type = 'relief' AND visibility = 'public' AND status = 'active' AND deleted_at IS NULL;
欄位說明
owner_id建立者；relief 據點為建立該據點的組織成員
org_idrelief 必填；其餘類型為 NULL
type / status / visibility見枚舉。visibility 有效值為 public / friends（預設）/ selected / private（D51 移除 link / password），public 不再限 relief：任何類型皆可 public（列於擁有者個人頁）；relief 據點仍須組織已驗證才能 public（wishlists_check_org_verified trigger 不變）。語意見 可見性矩陣
access_password_hashD51（0016）：欄位與 CHECK wishlists_password_has_hash 已移除
slug10 碼 base62 隨機字串，UNIQUE，對外分享用；改 slug 不提供
event_date活動日（生日 / 預產期 / 婚期）；surprise_mode 需設定；unlock_at = event_date 當日 00:00 Asia/Taipei，此刻起解鎖
show_claimer_namestrue 時其他訪客可看捐助者暱稱；relief 強制 false
surprise_mode驚喜模式；relief 不可開
locationgeography(Point,4326)，relief 必填；經度在前（lng, lat）
address據點地址文字
site_statusactive | paused | closed；僅 relief
claim_ttl_hours（D7）捐助保留時數；NULL = 不逾期（personal / registry 預設）；relief 建立時預設 48。claims.expires_at = 建立時間 + 此值，轉 purchased 後清除
closes_at自動結束時間，選填，預設 NULL（不設期限）。到期後捐助 / 認捐即時回 WISHLIST_CLOSED，背景 job 每 5 分鐘把 active 轉 closed，創建者可改期或清除後重開；到期前 24 小時寄一次提醒（closes_reminded_at）
deleted_at軟刪除；公開查詢一律加 deleted_at IS NULL
moderation_status / moderation_reason / moderated_at / moderated_byok | hidden；hidden 時公開頁與 SSE 回 410 WISHLIST_REMOVED，建立者在 dashboard 看到原因；hidden 必須有 reason（CHECK）；staff 動作寫 audit_logs
cover_image_status封面圖的處理狀態，語意同 wishlist_items.image_status

merchants

-- ---------- merchants / catalog_products（P2：平台商品目錄）----------
CREATE TABLE merchants (
  id          uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  name        text        NOT NULL,
  api_type    text        NOT NULL CHECK (api_type IN ('rest_api', 'manual')),  -- rest_api：自動下單；manual：人工通知
  active      boolean     NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
  created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
欄位說明
api_typerest_api（平台自動下單）| manual（人工通知商家）
active停用後其商品不可新增為眾籌品項

catalog_products

CREATE TABLE catalog_products (
  id            uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  merchant_id   uuid        NOT NULL REFERENCES merchants(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  title         text        NOT NULL,
  image_key     text,
  price_amount  bigint      NOT NULL CHECK (price_amount >= 0),          -- 新台幣元（= 點數）
  shipping_fee_amount bigint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (shipping_fee_amount >= 0),
  stock_status  text        NOT NULL DEFAULT 'in_stock' CHECK (stock_status IN ('in_stock', 'low', 'out_of_stock')),
  merchant_sku  text        NOT NULL,
  active        boolean     NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
  created_at    timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at    timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  UNIQUE (merchant_id, merchant_sku)
);
CREATE INDEX catalog_products_active_idx ON catalog_products (active, title);

-- [舊草案，以 0041 的 wishlists_relief_publish 取代] 未驗證組織不可建立 public 據點（D43）；應用層將此例外映射為 403 ORG_NOT_VERIFIED
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION wishlists_check_org_verified() RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
BEGIN
  IF NEW.type = 'relief' AND NEW.visibility = 'public' AND NOT EXISTS (
       SELECT 1 FROM organizations o WHERE o.id = NEW.org_id AND o.verification_status = 'verified') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'ORG_NOT_VERIFIED' USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $$;
CREATE TRIGGER wishlists_org_verified_trg BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OF visibility, org_id, type ON wishlists
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION wishlists_check_org_verified();
欄位說明
price_amount / shipping_fee_amount新台幣元（= 點數）；建立品項時快照到 price_snapshot_amount
stock_statusin_stock | low | out_of_stock；out_of_stock 不可選
merchant_sku商家端 SKU；(merchant_id, merchant_sku) UNIQUE
active下架後既有進行中的品項不受影響，但無法新增

content_reports

-- ---------- content_reports（D35：檢舉，MVP）----------
CREATE TABLE content_reports (
  id                uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  wishlist_id       uuid          NOT NULL REFERENCES wishlists(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  item_id           uuid,         -- 選填：被檢舉的品項（不設 FK，品項軟刪除後報告仍保留）
  reason            report_reason NOT NULL,
  detail            text          CHECK (char_length(detail) <= 1000),
  reporter_guest_id uuid          REFERENCES guests(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  reporter_user_id  uuid          REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  status            report_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open',
  handled_by        uuid          REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  handled_at        timestamptz,
  created_at        timestamptz   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  CONSTRAINT reports_single_reporter CHECK (reporter_guest_id IS NULL OR reporter_user_id IS NULL),
  CONSTRAINT reports_handled_consistent CHECK ((status = 'open') = (handled_at IS NULL))
);
CREATE INDEX content_reports_queue_idx ON content_reports (status, created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX content_reports_wishlist_idx ON content_reports (wishlist_id, created_at DESC);
欄位說明
reasonscam | inappropriate | copyright | personal_info | other
reporter_guest_id / reporter_user_id檢舉者（可皆為 NULL = 匿名）；至多一個（CHECK）
item_id選填：被檢舉品項；不設 FK，品項軟刪除後報告仍保留
statusopen → actioned | dismissed；handled_at 與 status 一致（CHECK）
（處理）staff 於 /admin 檢舉佇列處理；下架（moderation_status=hidden）時同清單 open 檢舉一併 actioned

wishlist_items

-- ---------- wishlist_items ----------
CREATE TABLE wishlist_items (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  wishlist_id      uuid          NOT NULL REFERENCES wishlists(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  title            text          NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(title) BETWEEN 1 AND 120),
  description      text,
  brand            text,
  spec             text,
  image_key        text,
  image_status     image_status  NOT NULL DEFAULT 'none',   -- none | pending（已上傳待處理）| ready | rejected
  product_url      text,
  unit_price_amount bigint       CHECK (unit_price_amount >= 0),
  funding_mode     funding_mode  NOT NULL DEFAULT 'quantity',
  priority         item_priority NOT NULL DEFAULT 'medium',
  category         relief_category,                -- relief 才有
  urgency          relief_urgency,                 -- relief 才有
  qty_needed       integer       NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  qty_claimed      integer       NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,   -- 快取
  qty_received     integer       NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  -- ---- crowdfund（P2，點數）----
  target_points    bigint,                              -- = 商品價 + 運費 + 服務費 / 緩衝
  pledged_points   bigint        NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,   -- 快取：pledged + captured 的 contributions.points 加總
  funding_status   funding_status,                      -- quantity 為 NULL；crowdfund 建立時 'open'
  funding_deadline timestamptz,                         -- 預設 event_date 23:59 Asia/Taipei
  fulfillment_type fulfillment_type,                    -- catalog 平台目錄自動下單 / concierge 人工代購
  catalog_product_id uuid        REFERENCES catalog_products(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  price_snapshot_amount bigint   CHECK (price_snapshot_amount >= 0),  -- 建立時的商品價（含運）快照，新台幣元
  expired_at       timestamptz,                         -- 轉為 expired 的時間；7 天選擇期自此起算
  sort_order       integer       NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  created_at       timestamptz   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at       timestamptz   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  deleted_at       timestamptz,
  CONSTRAINT items_image_status_key    CHECK ((image_status <> 'none' OR image_key IS NULL) AND (image_status NOT IN ('pending', 'ready') OR image_key IS NOT NULL)),
  CONSTRAINT items_qty_needed_pos      CHECK (qty_needed > 0),
  CONSTRAINT items_qty_claimed_range   CHECK (qty_claimed >= 0 AND qty_claimed <= qty_needed),
  CONSTRAINT items_qty_received_nonneg CHECK (qty_received >= 0),
  CONSTRAINT items_points_nonneg       CHECK (pledged_points >= 0),
  CONSTRAINT items_crowdfund_shape     CHECK (funding_mode <> 'crowdfund' OR (
      qty_needed = 1 AND target_points IS NOT NULL AND target_points > 0
      AND fulfillment_type IS NOT NULL AND funding_status IS NOT NULL AND funding_deadline IS NOT NULL)),
  CONSTRAINT items_catalog_requires_product CHECK (fulfillment_type IS DISTINCT FROM 'catalog' OR catalog_product_id IS NOT NULL),
  CONSTRAINT items_quantity_no_funding CHECK (funding_mode <> 'quantity' OR (
      target_points IS NULL AND pledged_points = 0 AND funding_status IS NULL AND funding_deadline IS NULL
      AND fulfillment_type IS NULL AND catalog_product_id IS NULL AND price_snapshot_amount IS NULL AND expired_at IS NULL)),
  CONSTRAINT items_expired_has_time    CHECK ((funding_status = 'expired') = (expired_at IS NOT NULL) OR funding_status IS NULL),
  CONSTRAINT items_crowdfund_no_claims CHECK (funding_mode <> 'crowdfund' OR qty_claimed = 0),
  -- 硬上限：絕不超額
  CONSTRAINT items_pledged_le_target   CHECK (target_points IS NULL OR pledged_points <= target_points),
  CONSTRAINT items_funded_is_full      CHECK (funding_status IS NULL OR funding_status NOT IN ('funded', 'fulfilled') OR pledged_points = target_points)
);
CREATE INDEX items_wishlist_idx ON wishlist_items (wishlist_id, sort_order) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
-- relief 需求彙總
CREATE INDEX items_relief_summary_idx ON wishlist_items (category, urgency) WHERE category IS NOT NULL AND deleted_at IS NULL;
-- 尚未捐助滿的品項（儀表板 / 彙總）
-- 截止 job：找出已到期仍 open 的眾籌品項
CREATE INDEX items_funding_deadline_idx ON wishlist_items (funding_deadline) WHERE funding_status IN ('open', 'expired');
CREATE INDEX items_open_idx ON wishlist_items (wishlist_id) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL AND qty_claimed < qty_needed;

-- relief 清單的品項必須有 category + urgency（D62 起 quantity / crowdfund 皆可）；非 relief 不可有。實作為 0041 的 items_relief_shape
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION wishlist_items_validate() RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
DECLARE t wishlist_type;
BEGIN
  SELECT type INTO t FROM wishlists WHERE id = NEW.wishlist_id;
  IF t = 'relief' THEN
    IF NEW.category IS NULL OR NEW.urgency IS NULL THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'relief item requires category and urgency' USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation';
    END IF;
    -- [D62：已取消此限制，relief 品項 quantity / crowdfund 皆可；下面三行為舊草案]
    IF false AND NEW.funding_mode <> 'quantity' THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'relief item must use quantity funding_mode' USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation';
    END IF;
  ELSIF NEW.category IS NOT NULL OR NEW.urgency IS NOT NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'category/urgency only allowed on relief items' USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $$;
CREATE TRIGGER wishlist_items_validate_trg
  BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OF wishlist_id, category, urgency, funding_mode ON wishlist_items
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION wishlist_items_validate();
欄位說明
image_statusnone | pending（已上傳待處理）| ready | rejected（D47-6）；EXIF 處理 job 完成前公開頁不顯示圖片（image_url 為 null）。CHECK：none 時 image_key 必為 NULL；pending / ready 時必須有 image_key；rejected 時 image_key 清空
funding_modequantity | crowdfund。MVP（P0/P1）建立品項只能選 quantity；crowdfund 為 P2。已有捐助 / 認捐後不可變更
priorityitem_priority，personal 常用；預設 medium
category / urgencyrelief 專用，由 trigger 強制；非 relief 必須為 NULL
unit_price_amount預估單價（新台幣元），僅供顯示
qty_needed需求數量；crowdfund 固定 1
qty_claimed快取：reserved+purchased+delivered 的 claims.qty 加總；CHECK <= qty_needed
qty_receivedrelief 實收（receipts.qty 加總）；registry 可視為 delivered 數，由應用層維護
target_pointscrowdfund 必填且 > 0（= 商品價 + 運費 + 服務費 / 緩衝）；quantity 必須 NULL
pledged_points快取：status in (pledged, captured) 的 contributions.points 加總；CHECK pledged_points <= target_points（硬上限，無超額）
funding_statusopen | funded | expired | fulfilled；quantity 為 NULL。funded 時 pledged_points 必須等於 target_points（CHECK）
funding_deadline募集截止；預設 event_date 23:59 Asia/Taipei；crowdfund 必填
fulfillment_typecatalog（平台商品目錄自動下單）| concierge（平台人工代購）；crowdfund 必填
catalog_product_idfulfillment_type=catalog 時必填（CHECK）
price_snapshot_amount建立時的商品價（含運）快照，新台幣元，供計算 target_points 與事後對帳
expired_at轉為 expired 的時間；7 天選擇期自此起算（now < expired_at + 7d）。CHECK：funding_status=expired ⇔ expired_at 不為 NULL
sort_order排序，reorder API 以 10 為間距重寫

claims

-- ---------- claims（數量型捐助）----------
CREATE TABLE claims (
  id            uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  item_id       uuid         NOT NULL REFERENCES wishlist_items(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  guest_id      uuid         REFERENCES guests(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  user_id       uuid         REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  claimer_name  text         NOT NULL,                 -- 捐助當下暱稱快照
  qty           integer      NOT NULL CHECK (qty > 0),
  status        claim_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'reserved',
  note          text         CHECK (char_length(note) <= 200),
  expires_at    timestamptz,
  purchased_at  timestamptz,
  delivered_at  timestamptz,
  cancelled_at  timestamptz,
  visibility    share_level,                -- v0.5：NULL = 訪客捐助（沿用 show_claimer_names 舊行為）；登入者必有值（trigger 套帳號預設）
  created_at    timestamptz  NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at    timestamptz  NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  -- 帳號刪除 / 訪客清除後兩者皆可為 NULL，故只禁止「兩者同時有值」
  CONSTRAINT claims_single_actor CHECK (guest_id IS NULL OR user_id IS NULL),
  CONSTRAINT claims_visibility_user CHECK ((user_id IS NULL) OR (visibility IS NOT NULL))   -- v0.5
);
-- v0.5：登入者捐助未指定 visibility → 套用 users.default_claim_visibility（BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OF user_id ON claims，claims_default_visibility()）
CREATE INDEX claims_item_idx ON claims (item_id);
-- 同一 guest / user 對同一品項只能有一筆有效捐助（改數量請用 PATCH）
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX claims_item_guest_active_uk ON claims (item_id, guest_id)
  WHERE guest_id IS NOT NULL AND status IN ('reserved', 'purchased', 'delivered');
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX claims_item_user_active_uk ON claims (item_id, user_id)
  WHERE user_id IS NOT NULL AND status IN ('reserved', 'purchased', 'delivered');
CREATE INDEX claims_item_active_idx ON claims (item_id) WHERE status IN ('reserved', 'purchased', 'delivered');
CREATE INDEX claims_guest_idx ON claims (guest_id) WHERE guest_id IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX claims_user_idx ON claims (user_id) WHERE user_id IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX claims_expiry_idx ON claims (expires_at) WHERE status = 'reserved' AND expires_at IS NOT NULL;
欄位說明
guest_id / user_id捐助者，二擇一（登入使用者也可捐助別人的清單）；帳號 / 訪客清除後皆可為 NULL，捐助保留
claimer_name捐助當下暱稱快照；PATCH /guest/me 改暱稱時同步更新
(item_id, guest_id) / (item_id, user_id)（D6）partial UNIQUE（status in reserved/purchased/delivered）：同一人對同一品項只能有一筆有效捐助，重複 POST → CLAIM_ALREADY_EXISTS，改數量用 PATCH
qty>0；變更數量以差額做條件式 UPDATE
statusreserved → purchased → delivered；reserved/purchased → cancelled；reserved → expired
visibilityv0.5：share_level（public | friends | private）。NULL = 訪客捐助，無公開層級設定、不出現在任何個人頁，分享頁沿用清單 show_claimer_names；登入者捐助必有值（建立時可指定，否則 trigger 取帳號預設；PATCH 可改）。分享頁上非 public 的登入者捐助一律顯示「匿名」；個人頁依 viewer 判斷（本人 / public / friends + 好友）
expires_at由 wishlists.claim_ttl_hours 計算；NULL = 不逾期；purchased 後清除；背景 job（/internal/jobs/tick）將逾期的 reserved 轉 expired 並回補 qty_claimed

social（好友・指定名單・存取密碼）

-- ---------- v0.5 0005_social：好友、指定名單（欄位 / enum 變更見 users、wishlists、claims）----------
-- 指定名單：visibility = 'selected' 時，僅名單內的登入使用者可看（名單成員必須是擁有者的好友，由 API 檢查）
CREATE TABLE wishlist_allowed_users (
  wishlist_id uuid        NOT NULL REFERENCES wishlists(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_id     uuid        NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  PRIMARY KEY (wishlist_id, user_id)
);
CREATE INDEX wishlist_allowed_users_user_idx ON wishlist_allowed_users (user_id);

-- 好友：無向，一列一對，user_a < user_b
CREATE TABLE friendships (
  user_a     uuid        NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_b     uuid        NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  PRIMARY KEY (user_a, user_b),
  CONSTRAINT friendships_ordered CHECK (user_a < user_b)
);
CREATE INDEX friendships_b_idx ON friendships (user_b);

-- 好友申請（以 email 或 handle 指定對象）；接受後刪除並寫入 friendships
CREATE TABLE friend_requests (
  id         uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  from_user  uuid        NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  to_user    uuid        NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  CONSTRAINT friend_requests_not_self CHECK (from_user <> to_user),
  UNIQUE (from_user, to_user)
);
CREATE INDEX friend_requests_to_idx ON friend_requests (to_user, created_at DESC);

-- 好友邀請連結 /invite/{token}（可 QR）：點開並登入即互為好友；可多次使用直到過期或撤銷
CREATE TABLE friend_invites (
  id         uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  user_id    uuid        NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  token_hash bytea       NOT NULL UNIQUE,   -- SHA-256(token)
  expires_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
  revoked_at timestamptz,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
CREATE INDEX friend_invites_user_idx ON friend_invites (user_id, created_at DESC);
表 / 欄位說明
wishlist_allowed_users指定名單；清單刪除連帶刪除。解除好友時，雙方清單的名單中彼此都會被移除（DELETE /friends/{user_id}）。名單內容不隨 visibility 切換清除（切走再切回 selected 仍在）
friendships無向、一列一對（user_a < user_b）；查詢時以 LEAST / GREATEST 正規化。MVP 的好友關係不需對方同意以外的任何條件：申請被接受、或對方也已申請（互相申請）、或使用邀請連結，即成立
friend_requests待處理申請；UNIQUE (from_user, to_user)；成為好友（任一路徑）時雙向申請都刪除
friend_invites邀請連結；DB 只存 SHA-256；有效期 7 天；每位使用者同時只有一組有效連結（重新產生會撤銷舊的）；可被多人使用直到過期或撤銷

point_wallets

-- ---------- point_wallets / point_ledger（P2：點數錢包）----------
-- 1 點 = NT$1。點數只能投入心願品項（達標後由平台購買）；不可轉讓、不可提領給受捐者。
-- balance 為快取 = Σ point_lots.remaining_points，並與 point_ledger 加總一致（每日對帳）。
CREATE TABLE point_wallets (
  id          uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  user_id     uuid        NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  balance     bigint      NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  status      wallet_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',   -- frozen：拒付 / 風控凍結，認捐 / 兌換 / 退款 → 403 WALLET_FROZEN
  created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  CONSTRAINT point_wallets_balance_nonneg CHECK (balance >= 0)
);
欄位說明
user_idUNIQUE，每位使用者一個錢包（首次儲值 / 兌換禮物卡時建立）
balance快取 = Σ point_lots.remaining_points（CHECK >= 0）；每日對帳。不含認捐佔用中的點數
（規則）點數永遠不能提領成現金給受捐者；只能投入心願品項（達標後由平台購買）。不可轉讓、錢包之間不可轉點
statusactive | frozen（D41）：拒付 / 風控凍結後，認捐 / 兌換 / 現金退款 → 403 WALLET_FROZEN；爭議勝訴後恢復 active

point_lots

實作狀態（D55，2026-10-19）：已落地（migration 0022）。point_lot_source 含 topup | gift_card | reward_bonus | adjustment（後兩者之外的 gift_card / reward_bonus 目前沒有對應功能，只有 enum 值與扣點順序）。post 依 kind 建批次：topup → source=topup（可退現金，(source, source_ref_id) 唯一防重複入帳）；grant / 其他正數 → 每筆一個 source=adjustment 批次（不可退現金，CHECK 強制）。扣點順序為 reward_bonus（最早到期）→ gift_card → adjustment → topup（與下方 4.3b 範例不同：把人工發放點數排在儲值之前，以保護可退現金的點數）；一筆 pledge 依批次拆成多列 ledger（各帶 lot_id），point_ledger_ref_uk 改為含 lot_id。退點（release / refund）依 contribution_lot_allocations 逆序（topup → adjustment → gift_card → reward_bonus）退回原批次；轉投時分配跟著新認捐。過期 reward_bonus 作廢尚未實作（無此功能）。回填：每個有歷史餘額或未結認捐（pledged / captured 尚未退回的部分）的錢包建一個舊資料批次（source=adjustment、source_ref_id = wallet id、不可退現金，original = balance + 認捐中點數，remaining = balance），其歷史 ledger 沒有 lot_id（帳本只增不改），所以舊批次只對帳錢包層級。對帳不變式：Σ lots.remaining = balance；每個（非舊資料）批次的 remaining = 該批次 ledger 加總。GET /wallet 的 wallet 另帶 cash_refundable_balance 與 lots（remaining > 0 的批次摘要）。
-- 點數批次（D23）：錢包內的點數依來源分批，來源決定能否退現金與是否到期
CREATE TABLE point_lots (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  wallet_id        uuid             NOT NULL REFERENCES point_wallets(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  source           point_lot_source NOT NULL,
  source_ref_id    uuid,            -- topup.id / gift_cards.id / reward_offers.id / 人工調整單
  original_points  bigint           NOT NULL CHECK (original_points > 0),
  remaining_points bigint           NOT NULL,
  cash_refundable  boolean          NOT NULL,
  expires_at       timestamptz,
  created_at       timestamptz      NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  CONSTRAINT point_lots_remaining_range CHECK (remaining_points >= 0 AND remaining_points <= original_points),
  CONSTRAINT point_lots_source_rules CHECK (CASE source
      WHEN 'topup'        THEN cash_refundable AND expires_at IS NULL                       -- 儲值：可退現金、不設期限
      WHEN 'gift_card'    THEN NOT cash_refundable AND expires_at IS NULL                   -- 禮物卡兌入：不可退現金、不設期限
      WHEN 'reward_bonus' THEN NOT cash_refundable AND expires_at IS NOT NULL               -- 星星兌換的贊助點數：不可退現金、90 天到期
      ELSE true END)
);
CREATE INDEX point_lots_wallet_idx ON point_lots (wallet_id, source, expires_at) WHERE remaining_points > 0;
CREATE INDEX point_lots_expiry_idx ON point_lots (expires_at) WHERE source = 'reward_bonus' AND remaining_points > 0;
-- 同一來源只能入帳一次（儲值 webhook 重送、禮物卡重複兌換）
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX point_lots_source_uk ON point_lots (source, source_ref_id) WHERE source IN ('topup', 'gift_card');
欄位說明
sourcetopup 儲值 | gift_card 禮物卡兌入 | reward_bonus 星星兌換的平台贊助點數 | adjustment 人工調整
original_points / remaining_points建立時點數 / 剩餘點數（CHECK 0 <= remaining <= original）；所有異動皆同步寫 ledger（含 lot_id），因此 remaining = 該批次 ledger 加總
cash_refundabletopup = true；gift_card、reward_bonus = false（CHECK 依 source 強制）。現金退款只能扣 cash_refundable 批次
expires_at僅 reward_bonus（兌換起 90 天）；topup、gift_card 不設期限（禮券規範）
source_ref_id來源單據：topups.id / gift_cards.id / reward_offers.id；topup 與 gift_card 以 (source, source_ref_id) 唯一，防重複入帳
（扣點順序）認捐：reward_bonus（最早到期優先）→ gift_card → topup；退點：逆序退回原批次，過期的 reward_bonus 該部分作廢

point_ledger

CREATE TABLE point_ledger (
  id             uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  seq            bigint             GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY,      -- 嚴格遞增，用於同毫秒內排序與對帳
  wallet_id      uuid               NOT NULL REFERENCES point_wallets(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  lot_id         uuid               REFERENCES point_lots(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,   -- 異動所屬批次（v0.3）
  delta          bigint             NOT NULL CHECK (delta <> 0),
  balance_after  bigint             NOT NULL CHECK (balance_after >= 0),
  entry_type     ledger_entry_type  NOT NULL,
  ref_type       text,              -- topup | contribution | purchase_order | cash_refund_request | gift_card | reward_offer | manual
  ref_id         uuid,
  created_at     timestamptz        NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
CREATE INDEX point_ledger_wallet_idx ON point_ledger (wallet_id, id DESC);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX point_ledger_seq_uk ON point_ledger (seq);
-- 防重複入帳：儲值、禮物卡兌換、認捐（pledge / release）、現金退款，同一來源同一批次只能記一次
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX point_ledger_ref_uk ON point_ledger (wallet_id, entry_type, ref_type, ref_id, lot_id)
  WHERE ref_type IN ('topup', 'contribution', 'cash_refund_request', 'gift_card');

-- 只增不改：禁止 UPDATE / DELETE
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION point_ledger_immutable() RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
BEGIN
  RAISE EXCEPTION 'point_ledger is append-only' USING ERRCODE = 'restrict_violation';
END $$;
CREATE TRIGGER point_ledger_no_update BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON point_ledger
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION point_ledger_immutable();
欄位說明
seq（補充）GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY，嚴格遞增；同毫秒內的排序與對帳用（UUID v7 不保證毫秒內單調）
lot_id異動所屬批次；一次認捐跨多批次會有多筆
delta正負；不可為 0
balance_after異動後 wallet 餘額；同錢包 seq 最大的一筆必等於 point_wallets.balance
entry_typetopup | pledge | release | cash_refund | adjustment | gift_claim（禮物卡兌入）| reward_bonus（星星兌換）| expire（贊助點數到期 / 作廢退點）
ref_type / ref_id來源：topup、contribution、purchase_order、cash_refund_request、gift_card、reward_offer、point_lot、manual
（不可變）trigger 禁止 UPDATE / DELETE；更正以反向分錄為之
point_ledger_ref_uk部分唯一索引，鍵為 (wallet_id, entry_type, ref_type, ref_id, lot_id)（D31-4）：一筆認捐可從多個批次扣點，每個批次一筆 ledger；topup / contribution / cash_refund_request / gift_card 來源同一批次只能記一次，防重複入帳

invoices

-- ---------- invoices（D42：電子發票，P2-A）----------
CREATE TABLE invoices (
  id             uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  source_type    invoice_source       NOT NULL,
  source_id      uuid                 NOT NULL,          -- topups.id / gift_card_orders.id / gift_card_batches.id
  invoice_no     text                 UNIQUE,            -- 開立後才有
  amount         bigint               NOT NULL CHECK (amount > 0),
  tax_type       text                 NOT NULL DEFAULT 'taxable' CHECK (tax_type IN ('taxable', 'zero_rate', 'tax_free')),
  status         invoice_status       NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
  carrier_type   invoice_carrier_type NOT NULL DEFAULT 'member',
  carrier_id     text,                                   -- 手機條碼 / 自然人憑證
  donate_code    text,                                   -- 捐贈碼
  buyer_tax_id   char(8),                                -- 公司統編（b2b）
  buyer_name     text,
  issued_at      timestamptz,
  voided_at      timestamptz,
  created_at     timestamptz          NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at     timestamptz          NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  CONSTRAINT invoices_carrier_fields CHECK (
    (carrier_type <> 'mobile_barcode' OR carrier_id IS NOT NULL) AND
    (carrier_type <> 'citizen_cert'   OR carrier_id IS NOT NULL) AND
    (carrier_type <> 'donate'         OR donate_code IS NOT NULL) AND
    (carrier_type <> 'b2b'            OR (buyer_tax_id IS NOT NULL AND buyer_tax_id ~ '^[0-9]{8}$'))),
  CONSTRAINT invoices_issued_has_no CHECK (status NOT IN ('issued', 'allowance') OR (invoice_no IS NOT NULL AND issued_at IS NOT NULL))
);
-- 同一來源同時只能有一張有效發票（作廢 / 折讓後可重開）
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX invoices_source_active_uk ON invoices (source_type, source_id) WHERE status IN ('pending', 'issued');
CREATE INDEX invoices_source_idx ON invoices (source_type, source_id);
欄位說明
source_type / source_idtopup | gift_card_order | gift_card_batch | service_fee 與其單據 id；同一來源同時只能有一張 pending / issued 發票（部分唯一索引）
invoice_no開立後才有（UNIQUE）；status=issued / allowance 必須有 invoice_no 與 issued_at（CHECK）
carrier_typemember（會員載具，預設）| mobile_barcode | citizen_cert | donate | b2b；對應欄位必填（CHECK）
carrier_id / donate_code / buyer_tax_id / buyer_name載具號碼、捐贈碼、公司統編（8 碼）與抬頭；屬個資，匯出與刪除遵守 D40，發票本身依商業會計法保存
statuspending → issued；issued → void（作廢）或 allowance（折讓，現金退款時）
（時點）開立時點（儲值 / 購買時 vs 兌換 / 下單時）待會計師確認；本表兩者皆可支援

topups

實作狀態（D54，2026-10-19，階段 1）：topups / invoices 已依本節收斂（migration 0021），主要 provider 為 ecpay（綠界 AioCheckOut，見 PRD 決策 D54）。與下表的差異：新增 merchant_trade_no（我方訂單編號，UNIQUE，T 開頭、≤20 字元）與 expires_at；method 除 card 外實作 cvs_code | cvs_barcode | atm（line_pay | apple_pay 回 422 UNSUPPORTED）；status 啟用 pending | paid | failed | expired（refunded | disputed | charged_back 尚未使用）；尚無 point_lots、拒付與自動儲值，tappay 未實作。端點：POST /api/v1/wallet/topups（Idempotency-Key 必填；amount 100–50,000；單一使用者每小時最多 10 筆，超過 429 RATE_LIMITED 帶 Retry-After；錢包含待付款不得超過 NT$50,000，否則 409 WALLET_CAP_EXCEEDED；新帳號每日上限 409 TOPUP_LIMIT_EXCEEDED，目前只計儲值單；回 201 payment.type = form_post）與 POST /api/v1/webhooks/payments/{provider}（ecpay：CheckMacValue 驗簽，錯誤回 401 UNAUTHORIZED；鎖訂單列、RtnCode=1 且 TradeAmt 相符才入帳；金額不符回 422 AMOUNT_MISMATCH 並寫 audit_logs；已處理重送、RtnCode≠1 皆回純文字 1|OK；逾期（expired）後才付款仍入帳；tappay 回 501 PROVIDER_NOT_IMPLEMENTED）。背景工作每分鐘把逾期 pending 標 expired。ECPAY_* 未設齊時兩端點回 503 TOPUP_DISABLED。D56 已補：QueryTradeInfo 二次確認（入帳前、交易外）、PaymentInfoURL 取號通知（POST /webhooks/payments/{provider}/info，欄位 payment_info，migration 0025）、GET /wallet/topups[/{id}]。尚未做：GET /config/public 的 topup 欄位、tappay。前端見 /me/topup。
CREATE TABLE topups (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  wallet_id        uuid             NOT NULL REFERENCES point_wallets(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  amount           bigint           NOT NULL CHECK (amount > 0),     -- 新台幣元 = 入帳點數
  method           topup_method     NOT NULL,
  provider         payment_provider NOT NULL,                       -- 由後端依 method 與設定決定
  provider_txn_id  text             UNIQUE,                          -- 供應商交易編號（webhook 冪等鍵）；建立時可為 NULL
  is_auto          boolean          NOT NULL DEFAULT false,         -- 自動儲值觸發（計入月上限）
  status           topup_status     NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
  invoice_id       uuid             REFERENCES invoices(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  failure_reason   text,
  payment_info     jsonb,                                           -- 取號資訊（D56，migration 0025）：綠界 PaymentInfoURL 通知寫入；card 為 NULL
  paid_at          timestamptz,
  created_at       timestamptz      NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at       timestamptz      NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  CONSTRAINT topups_paid_has_txn CHECK (status <> 'paid' OR (provider_txn_id IS NOT NULL AND paid_at IS NOT NULL))
);
CREATE INDEX topups_wallet_idx ON topups (wallet_id, created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX topups_pending_idx ON topups (created_at) WHERE status = 'pending';
欄位說明
methodcard | line_pay | apple_pay（前端傳入，補充枚舉 topup_method）
provider / provider_txn_idprovider（tappay | ecpay）由後端依 method 與設定決定，前端不傳；provider_txn_id UNIQUE，webhook 冪等鍵（建立時可為 NULL）
is_auto自動儲值觸發的儲值單；計入 wallet_auto_topups.monthly_cap_amount 當月上限
amount新台幣元 = 入帳點數；實際付款金額與 webhook 回報不符時不入帳
statuspending | paid | failed | refunded；paid 必須有 provider_txn_id 與 paid_at（CHECK）
status（v0.4）pending | paid | failed | refunded | disputed（爭議中，錢包凍結）| charged_back（拒付敗訴，扣回批次、不足記 wallet_debts）
invoice_id關聯 invoices（D42，取代原 invoice_no）；儲值頁的發票選項在建立儲值單時寫入
payment_infojsonb，D56。超商 / ATM 取號成功後由 PaymentInfoURL 通知寫入，對外 JSON 即此結構：cvs_code → {type, payment_no, expire_at}；cvs_barcode → {type, barcode1, barcode2, barcode3, expire_at}；atm → {type, bank_code, account, expire_at}。取號前與信用卡為 NULL；只在 pending 時寫入

cash_refund_requests

CREATE TABLE cash_refund_requests (
  id              uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  wallet_id       uuid                  NOT NULL REFERENCES point_wallets(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  amount          bigint                NOT NULL CHECK (amount > 0),
  status          refund_request_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'requested',
  lot_allocations jsonb                 NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,   -- 扣自哪些 cash_refundable 批次：[{lot_id, points}]；rejected 時據此退回原批次
  destination_enc bytea,                -- 退款帳戶資訊（應用層 AEAD 加密）；原路退回時為 NULL
  reject_reason   text,
  processed_by    uuid                  REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  completed_at    timestamptz,
  created_at      timestamptz           NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at      timestamptz           NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
CREATE INDEX cash_refund_requests_wallet_idx ON cash_refund_requests (wallet_id, created_at DESC);
欄位說明
amount自錢包餘額先扣（見 4.3h），僅未被認捐佔用的點數可申請
statusrequested → processing → completed；或 rejected（點數以 adjustment 退回）
lot_allocationsjsonb [{lot_id, points}]：申請時扣自哪些 cash_refundable 批次；rejected 時據此退回原批次
destination_enc退款帳戶（應用層 AEAD 加密）；原路退回時為 NULL
processed_by經手營運人員

wallet_debts

實作狀態（D55，2026-10-19）：表與營運端點已落地（migration 0024）。拒付處理 4.3s 以 library 函式提供，待金流 webhook 呼叫：wallet_ops::dispute_open(tx, topup_id)（paid → disputed，凍結錢包）、charge_back(tx, topup_id)（paid | disputed → charged_back；先扣回該 topup 批次剩餘點數並寫 adjustment ledger，不足部分記 wallet_debts，錢包凍結；回傳 ChargedBack::Applied { recovered, debt } 或 Ignored）、dispute_won(tx, topup_id)（disputed → paid，錢包恢復 active）。函式需在呼叫端交易內執行，先鎖 topups 列、再鎖錢包；重送冪等。未實作：禮物卡 void 與購買者封鎖（無禮物卡功能）；認捐中點數視為已花掉，不從 pledged 認捐追回。
CREATE TABLE wallet_debts (
  id          uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  wallet_id   uuid             NOT NULL REFERENCES point_wallets(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  topup_id    uuid             NOT NULL REFERENCES topups(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  amount      bigint           NOT NULL CHECK (amount > 0),       -- 拒付時已被花掉、無法扣回的點數價值；balance CHECK >= 0 不變
  status      wallet_debt_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open',
  note        text,
  resolved_by uuid             REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  resolved_at timestamptz,
  created_at  timestamptz      NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at  timestamptz      NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  UNIQUE (topup_id),
  CONSTRAINT wallet_debts_resolved_consistent CHECK ((status = 'open') = (resolved_at IS NULL))
);
CREATE INDEX wallet_debts_wallet_idx ON wallet_debts (wallet_id, status);
CREATE INDEX cash_refund_requests_open_idx ON cash_refund_requests (created_at) WHERE status IN ('requested', 'processing');
欄位說明
amount拒付時已被花掉、無法扣回的點數價值；balance CHECK >= 0 不變，欠款獨立記錄，平台吸收損失
statusopen | settled | written_off
topup_idUNIQUE：同一筆拒付只記一次欠款（重送 webhook 冪等）
note / resolved_by / resolved_at結案說明、經手營運人員與時間；status = open 時 resolved_at 必為 NULL（CHECK）；由 PATCH /admin/wallet-debts/{id} 結案

contributions

-- ---------- contributions（點數認捐；僅登入使用者）----------
CREATE TABLE contributions (
  id                      uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  item_id                 uuid                NOT NULL REFERENCES wishlist_items(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  wishlist_id             uuid                NOT NULL REFERENCES wishlists(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,   -- 冗餘；須與 item 所屬清單一致
  user_id                 uuid                NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  wallet_id               uuid                NOT NULL REFERENCES point_wallets(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  points                  bigint              NOT NULL CHECK (points > 0),
  refunded_points         bigint              NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,   -- 已退回錢包的點數（差額退回 / 購買失敗）；實際花費 = points - refunded_points
  status                  contribution_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pledged',
  message                 text                CHECK (char_length(message) <= 200),
  is_anonymous            boolean             NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  visibility              share_level         NOT NULL,   -- 未指定時由 trigger 套帳號預設
  reallocated_to_item_id  uuid                REFERENCES wishlist_items(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  captured_at             timestamptz,
  released_at             timestamptz,
  created_at              timestamptz         NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at              timestamptz         NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  CONSTRAINT contributions_refunded_range CHECK (refunded_points >= 0 AND refunded_points <= points),
  CONSTRAINT contributions_realloc_consistent CHECK ((status = 'reallocated') = (reallocated_to_item_id IS NOT NULL))
);
CREATE INDEX contributions_item_idx ON contributions (item_id);
CREATE INDEX contributions_item_active_idx ON contributions (item_id) WHERE status IN ('pledged', 'captured');
CREATE INDEX contributions_user_idx ON contributions (user_id, created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX contributions_wallet_idx ON contributions (wallet_id);
CREATE FUNCTION contributions_default_visibility() RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
BEGIN
  IF NEW.visibility IS NULL THEN
    SELECT default_claim_visibility INTO NEW.visibility FROM users WHERE id = NEW.user_id;
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $$;
CREATE TRIGGER contributions_default_visibility BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OF user_id ON contributions
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION contributions_default_visibility();
欄位說明
user_idNOT NULL：認捐需登入，訪客不能認捐（D50 起數量型捐助同樣需登入）
wallet_id扣點的錢包；與 user_id 對應
wishlist_id冗餘；須與 item 所屬清單一致，由應用層驗證
points>0；1 點 = NT$1
refunded_points（補充）已退回錢包的點數（差額退回 / 購買失敗）；實際花費 = points - refunded_points
statuspledged（已認捐，點數鎖定）→ captured（達標，點數用於購買）；pledged → released（撤回 / 逾期 / 失敗退回）；captured → released（購買失敗）；pledged → reallocated（截止後轉投）
message給受捐者的留言；驚喜鎖定期間建立者看不到
visibilitypublic | friends | private；建立時未指定由 trigger 套帳號預設，之後可由本人 PATCH。個人頁依逐筆值過濾，匿名仍僅本人可見；轉投複製原值
is_anonymoustrue 時連解鎖後建立者也只看到「匿名朋友」
reallocated_to_item_idstatus=reallocated 時指向轉投的目標品項（CHECK 兩者一致）；轉投另建一筆 pledged
captured_at / released_at狀態時間戳

contribution_lot_allocations

-- 認捐扣點分配（D23）：這筆認捐扣自哪些批次；released 時退回原批次（過期的 reward_bonus 批次該部分作廢）
CREATE TABLE contribution_lot_allocations (
  contribution_id  uuid   NOT NULL REFERENCES contributions(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  lot_id           uuid   NOT NULL REFERENCES point_lots(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  points           bigint NOT NULL CHECK (points > 0),
  refunded_points  bigint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  PRIMARY KEY (contribution_id, lot_id),
  CONSTRAINT allocations_refunded_range CHECK (refunded_points >= 0 AND refunded_points <= points)
);
CREATE INDEX contribution_lot_allocations_lot_idx ON contribution_lot_allocations (lot_id);
欄位說明
(contribution_id, lot_id)複合 PK；一筆認捐可扣自多個批次
points扣自該批次的點數
refunded_points已退回該批次的點數（撤回 / 差額退回 / 購買失敗）；過期 reward_bonus 的作廢部分也計入（不入帳）
（不變式）每筆 pledged / captured 認捐：points - refunded_points = Σ allocations.(points - refunded_points)

shipping_addresses

-- ---------- shipping_addresses（欄位級加密）----------
-- recipient_name_enc / phone_enc / address_enc 皆為應用層 AEAD（例如 XChaCha20-Poly1305 / AES-256-GCM）密文：
-- nonce || ciphertext || tag；金鑰由 KMS 管理，key_version 支援輪替。DB 層看不到明文。
CREATE TABLE shipping_addresses (
  id                  uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  wishlist_id         uuid        NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES wishlists(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  recipient_name_enc  bytea       NOT NULL,
  phone_enc           bytea       NOT NULL,
  address_enc         bytea       NOT NULL,
  key_version         smallint    NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  created_at          timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at          timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
欄位說明
wishlist_idUNIQUE，一份清單一組收件資訊；眾籌品項發佈前必須已填
recipient_name_enc / phone_enc / address_enc應用層 AEAD 密文（nonce || ciphertext || tag）；DB、備份、log 皆看不到明文；僅建立者（本人編輯）與履約作業（staff 下單）可解密，捐贈者永遠看不到
key_version加密金鑰版本，供 KMS 輪替

brands（品牌館，已實作 0028）

CREATE TABLE brands (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  slug text NOT NULL UNIQUE CHECK (slug ~ '^[a-z0-9-]{2,40}$'),
  name text NOT NULL, description text,
  logo_key text, logo_status image_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'none', website_url text,
  active boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false, sort_order int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(), updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
brand 是展示用品牌，不是 merchants。catalog_products 已於 0028 以下列欄位建立：id, ref（UNIQUE，= wishlist_items.catalog_ref，不可改）, brand_id（NULL = Wishly 精選，ON DELETE RESTRICT）, title, spec, note, price_amount, image_key, image_status, product_url, recipients text[], occasions text[], active（預設 false）, sort_order, created_at, updated_at；上方 DDL 的 merchant_id / merchant_sku / shipping_fee_amount / stock_status 由 P2-A 再以 ALTER 補上（既有列先回填再加 NOT NULL）。

purchase_orders

-- ---------- purchase_orders（達標後的採購單）----------
CREATE TABLE purchase_orders (
  id                        uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  item_id                   uuid             NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES wishlist_items(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,  -- 失敗重試時重用同一列
  fulfillment_type          fulfillment_type NOT NULL,
  merchant_id               uuid             REFERENCES merchants(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  merchant_order_id         text,
  amount                    bigint           NOT NULL CHECK (amount > 0),   -- 建立時 = target_points；下單後更新為實際花費（不得大於 target）
  status                    order_status     NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
  tracking_no               text,
  shipping_address_snapshot bytea            NOT NULL,                      -- 建單當下收件資訊快照（應用層 AEAD）
  failure_reason            text,
  attempts                  smallint         NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,            -- 自動下單重試次數
  next_attempt_at           timestamptz,                                    -- 下次重試時間（退避）
  locked_until              timestamptz,                                    -- job 租約：到期前其他 worker 不得領取
  operator_user_id          uuid             REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,  -- 人工代購經手人（is_staff）
  created_at                timestamptz      NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at                timestamptz      NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  CONSTRAINT orders_catalog_has_merchant CHECK (fulfillment_type <> 'catalog' OR merchant_id IS NOT NULL),
  CONSTRAINT orders_placed_has_ref CHECK (status NOT IN ('placed', 'shipped', 'delivered') OR merchant_order_id IS NOT NULL),
  CONSTRAINT orders_failed_has_reason CHECK (status <> 'failed' OR failure_reason IS NOT NULL)
);
CREATE INDEX purchase_orders_queue_idx ON purchase_orders (status, fulfillment_type, created_at) WHERE status IN ('pending', 'placed', 'shipped');
CREATE INDEX purchase_orders_retry_idx ON purchase_orders (next_attempt_at) WHERE status = 'pending' AND fulfillment_type = 'catalog';
欄位說明
item_idUNIQUE；購買失敗後重新達標時重用同一列（歷史記在 audit_logs）
amount建立時 = target_points（預算）；下單後更新為實際花費，不得大於 target_points
statuspending → placed → shipped → delivered；pending / placed → failed；營運取消為 cancelled（處理同 failed）
merchant_order_id / tracking_no商家訂單編號 / 物流單號；placed 起必填 merchant_order_id（CHECK）
shipping_address_snapshot建單當下收件資訊快照（應用層 AEAD），之後建立者改地址不影響已建單
operator_user_idconcierge 人工代購經手人（is_staff）
failure_reasonfailed 時必填（CHECK）
attempts / next_attempt_at / locked_until自動下單 job 的重試次數、下次重試時間（指數退避）與租約（locked_until 前其他 worker 不得領取）；達上限視為購買失敗

outbox_events

-- ---------- outbox_events（補充：交易內寫入，job 輪詢處理，確保「達標 → 下單」不遺失）----------
CREATE TABLE outbox_events (
  id            uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  kind          text        NOT NULL,          -- order.place / order.failed.notify / funding.expired.notify
  payload       jsonb       NOT NULL,
  attempts      smallint    NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  last_error    text,
  available_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  processed_at  timestamptz,
  created_at    timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
CREATE INDEX outbox_events_due_idx ON outbox_events (available_at) WHERE processed_at IS NULL;
欄位說明
（補充表）交易內寫入、job 輪詢處理，確保「達標 → 下單」「失敗 / 截止通知」不因程序當掉而遺失
kindorder.place / order.failed.notify / funding.expired.notify
processed_atNULL = 待處理；處理以 SELECT ... FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED 領取

wallet_auto_topups

-- ---------- wallet_auto_topups（D24：隨行卡式自動儲值，P2-A）----------
CREATE TABLE wallet_auto_topups (
  user_id            uuid PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  enabled            boolean          NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  threshold_points   bigint           NOT NULL DEFAULT 500 CHECK (threshold_points >= 0),   -- 認捐後餘額低於此值即觸發
  amount             bigint           NOT NULL DEFAULT 1000 CHECK (amount > 0),              -- 每次扣款金額（= 入帳點數）
  method             topup_method     NOT NULL DEFAULT 'card' CHECK (method = 'card'),
  provider           payment_provider NOT NULL DEFAULT 'tappay',
  card_token_ref     text,            -- 金流商 card token 參照，絕不存卡號
  card_last4         char(4),         -- 僅供顯示
  monthly_cap_amount bigint           NOT NULL DEFAULT 10000 CHECK (monthly_cap_amount > 0),
  created_at         timestamptz      NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at         timestamptz      NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  CONSTRAINT auto_topup_enabled_has_card CHECK (NOT enabled OR card_token_ref IS NOT NULL),
  CONSTRAINT auto_topup_amount_within_cap CHECK (amount <= monthly_cap_amount)
);
欄位說明
user_idPK，每位使用者一組設定
threshold_points / amount認捐後餘額低於 threshold 即觸發，每次扣款 amount（= 入帳點數）
method / provider目前僅 card；provider 由後端決定
card_token_ref / card_last4金流商 card token 參照，<strong>絕不存卡號</strong>；last4 僅供顯示。enabled 必須有 card_token_ref（CHECK）
monthly_cap_amount當月自動儲值上限，預設 10,000；amount 不得大於它（CHECK）。超過時 job 不扣並通知，不阻擋認捐

corporate_accounts

-- ---------- corporate_accounts / gift_card_batches（D27：企業禮券，P2-C）----------
CREATE TABLE corporate_accounts (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  name             text        NOT NULL,
  tax_id           char(8)     NOT NULL UNIQUE CHECK (tax_id ~ '^[0-9]{8}$'),   -- 統一編號
  contact_name     text        NOT NULL,
  contact_email    text        NOT NULL,
  billing_address  text        NOT NULL,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
欄位說明
tax_id8 碼統一編號，UNIQUE
contact_* / billing_address三聯式發票與聯絡用

gift_card_batches

CREATE TABLE gift_card_batches (
  id                    uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  corporate_account_id  uuid            NOT NULL REFERENCES corporate_accounts(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  quantity              integer         NOT NULL CHECK (quantity BETWEEN 1 AND 5000),
  face_value_points     bigint          NOT NULL CHECK (face_value_points > 0),
  total_amount          bigint          NOT NULL,
  status                gift_batch_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft',
  invoice_id            uuid            REFERENCES invoices(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,   -- 三聯式（含統編）
  paid_at               timestamptz,
  issued_at             timestamptz,
  codes_exported_at     timestamptz,    -- 兌換連結 CSV 只能匯出一次（補充）
  created_by_staff_id   uuid            NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  created_at            timestamptz     NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at            timestamptz     NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  CONSTRAINT batches_total_matches CHECK (total_amount = quantity::bigint * face_value_points),
  CONSTRAINT batches_issued_after_paid CHECK (status <> 'issued' OR (paid_at IS NOT NULL AND issued_at IS NOT NULL))
);
CREATE INDEX gift_card_batches_corp_idx ON gift_card_batches (corporate_account_id, created_at DESC);
欄位說明
quantity / face_value_points / total_amounttotal = quantity × face（CHECK）；quantity 1–5000
statusdraft → invoiced → paid → issued；或 void。issued 必須有 paid_at 與 issued_at（CHECK）
invoice_id關聯 invoices（source_type=gift_card_batch，三聯式含統編；取代原 invoice_no）
codes_exported_at兌換連結 CSV 匯出時間；非 NULL 後再匯出 → CODES_ALREADY_EXPORTED；補發（reissue）時重置為 NULL（D31-8）
created_by_staff_id建立的營運人員

gift_card_orders

-- ---------- gift_card_orders / gift_cards（D25：電子禮物卡，P2-B）----------
CREATE TABLE gift_card_orders (
  id                 uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  purchaser_user_id  uuid             NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  quantity           integer          NOT NULL CHECK (quantity BETWEEN 1 AND 10),
  face_value_points  bigint           NOT NULL CHECK (face_value_points > 0),   -- 面額由設定決定（300 / 500 / 1000 / 2000 / 3000）
  total_amount       bigint           NOT NULL,
  method             topup_method     NOT NULL,
  provider           payment_provider NOT NULL,
  provider_txn_id    text             UNIQUE,
  status             gift_order_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
  invoice_id         uuid             REFERENCES invoices(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  batch_id           uuid             REFERENCES gift_card_batches(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  paid_at            timestamptz,
  created_at         timestamptz      NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at         timestamptz      NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  CONSTRAINT gift_orders_total_matches CHECK (total_amount = quantity::bigint * face_value_points),
  CONSTRAINT gift_orders_paid_has_txn CHECK (status <> 'paid' OR (provider_txn_id IS NOT NULL AND paid_at IS NOT NULL))
);
CREATE INDEX gift_card_orders_purchaser_idx ON gift_card_orders (purchaser_user_id, created_at DESC);
欄位說明
purchaser_user_id購買者（需登入）
quantity / face_value_points / total_amount一次 1–10 張；面額由設定決定（300 / 500 / 1000 / 2000 / 3000）；total = quantity × face（CHECK）
method / provider / provider_txn_id付款資訊；provider_txn_id UNIQUE（webhook 冪等）
statuspending | paid | failed | refunded；paid 必須有 provider_txn_id 與 paid_at（CHECK）
batch_id（依裁決保留）關聯企業批次，一般個人訂單為 NULL
invoice_id關聯 invoices（取代原 invoice_no）

gift_cards

CREATE TABLE gift_cards (
  id                   uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  order_id             uuid             REFERENCES gift_card_orders(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  batch_id             uuid             REFERENCES gift_card_batches(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  code_hash            bytea            NOT NULL UNIQUE,   -- SHA-256(16 碼 Crockford base32 code)；code 只在產生時顯示一次
  code_enc             bytea,             -- AEAD 暫存密文（D31-1）：個人卡保留到 claimed / refunded / void 時清為 NULL（購買者可隨時重看連結）；企業卡於 CSV 匯出後清為 NULL
  face_value_points    bigint           NOT NULL CHECK (face_value_points > 0),
  theme                text             NOT NULL DEFAULT 'default',
  message              text             CHECK (char_length(message) <= 200),
  sender_display_name  text,
  status               gift_card_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
  claimed_by_user_id   uuid             REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  claimed_at           timestamptz,
  claimable_at         timestamptz,       -- 高風險訂單的延遲兌換時間（最多 24 小時）；NULL = 立即可兌換
  created_at           timestamptz      NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at           timestamptz      NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  CONSTRAINT gift_cards_enc_only_active CHECK (status = 'active' OR code_enc IS NULL),
  CONSTRAINT gift_cards_has_origin CHECK (order_id IS NOT NULL OR batch_id IS NOT NULL),
  CONSTRAINT gift_cards_claim_consistent CHECK ((status = 'claimed') = (claimed_by_user_id IS NOT NULL AND claimed_at IS NOT NULL))
);
CREATE INDEX gift_cards_order_idx ON gift_cards (order_id) WHERE order_id IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX gift_cards_batch_idx ON gift_cards (batch_id) WHERE batch_id IS NOT NULL;
欄位說明
code_hashSHA-256(16 碼 Crockford base32 code)，UNIQUE；code 只在產生時顯示一次
code_encAEAD 暫存密文（D31-1）：個人卡保留到 claimed / refunded / void 時清為 NULL，購買者可隨時用 GET /gift-cards/orders/{id} 重看分享連結；企業卡於 CSV 匯出後清為 NULL。CHECK：非 active 的卡 code_enc 必為 NULL
statusactive（可兌換）| claimed | refunded | void；claimed 必須有 claimed_by_user_id 與 claimed_at（CHECK）
order_id / batch_id個人購買 / 企業批次，至少一個（CHECK）
theme / message / sender_display_name卡面主題、祝福語（<= 200 字）、寄送者暱稱；兌換頁公開顯示
（規則）兌換後轉為 gift_card 點數批次：不可退現金、不到期、只能用於心願品項；未兌換可由購買者退款
claimable_at高風險訂單的延遲兌換時間（最多 24 小時）；NULL = 立即可兌換；未到時間兌換 → 409

reward_accounts

-- ---------- reward_accounts / star_ledger / reward_offers（D26：回饋星星，P2-B）----------
CREATE TABLE reward_accounts (
  user_id            uuid PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  stars_balance      bigint      NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  tier               reward_tier NOT NULL DEFAULT 'green',
  tier_evaluated_at  timestamptz,
  created_at         timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at         timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  CONSTRAINT reward_accounts_stars_nonneg CHECK (stars_balance >= 0)
);
欄位說明
stars_balance星星餘額，CHECK >= 0（扣回只扣到 0 為止）
tiergreen（預設）| gold（12 個月內累積 >= 300 星）；每日評估，tier_evaluated_at 記錄時間

star_ledger

CREATE TABLE star_ledger (
  id          uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  user_id     uuid        NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  delta       bigint      NOT NULL CHECK (delta <> 0),
  reason      star_reason NOT NULL,
  ref_type    text,       -- contribution | reward_offer | point_lot | manual
  ref_id      uuid,
  expires_at  timestamptz,  -- earn 列：賺取月份起算 12 個月
  created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
CREATE INDEX star_ledger_user_idx ON star_ledger (user_id, id DESC);
-- 同一筆認捐只賺一次、只扣回一次（job 重跑冪等）
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX star_ledger_ref_uk ON star_ledger (reason, ref_type, ref_id) WHERE reason IN ('earn', 'clawback');
欄位說明
delta正負，不為 0
reasonearn 賺取（captured 認捐，每 50 點 1 星）| redeem 兌換 | clawback 失敗扣回 | expire 到期
expires_at僅 earn 列：賺取月份起算 12 個月（月底到期）
star_ledger_ref_uk(reason, ref_type, ref_id) 部分唯一（earn / clawback）：同一筆認捐只賺一次、只扣回一次，job 重跑冪等

reward_offers

CREATE TABLE reward_offers (
  id             uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  stars_cost     integer     NOT NULL CHECK (stars_cost > 0),
  bonus_points   bigint      NOT NULL CHECK (bonus_points > 0),
  tier_required  reward_tier NOT NULL,        -- 僅此等級可兌換（green：100 星 = 50 點；gold：100 星 = 60 點）
  active         boolean     NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
  created_at     timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at     timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
INSERT INTO reward_offers (stars_cost, bonus_points, tier_required) VALUES (100, 50, 'green'), (100, 60, 'gold');
欄位說明
stars_cost / bonus_pointsgreen：100 星 = 50 點；gold：100 星 = 60 點（DDL 已預設兩列）
tier_required僅此等級可兌換；GET /rewards/offers 只回目前等級的方案
active下架即不可兌換

receipts

-- ---------- receipts（relief 據點實收登記；0041。不是捐款收據，D63）---------- ----------
CREATE TABLE receipts (
  id          uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  item_id     uuid        NOT NULL REFERENCES wishlist_items(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  wishlist_id uuid        NOT NULL REFERENCES wishlists(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  qty         int         NOT NULL CHECK (qty > 0),
  note        text        CHECK (char_length(note) <= 200),
  recorded_by uuid        NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
  created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
欄位說明
wishlist_id據點（冗餘）；必須與 item 所屬清單一致，由 POST /orgs/{id}/receipts 從 item 推導。
qty1 至 100000；品項 qty_received = SUM(qty)，實收可超過需求。
recorded_by登記的組織成員（volunteer 以上）。
（重送去重）無 client_request_id 欄位；重送由 Idempotency-Key 的 24 小時重放窗口處理。離線佇列延後。

idempotency_keys

-- ---------- idempotency_keys ----------
CREATE TABLE idempotency_keys (
  key             uuid        NOT NULL,
  scope           text        NOT NULL,        -- 例：guest:<guest_id>:POST /items/{id}/claims
  request_hash    bytea       NOT NULL,        -- SHA-256(method + path + body)
  response_status integer,                     -- NULL = 處理中
  response_body   jsonb,
  created_at      timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  PRIMARY KEY (scope, key)
);
CREATE INDEX idempotency_keys_created_idx ON idempotency_keys (created_at);  -- 24h 清除 job
欄位說明
(scope, key)複合 PK；scope = 主體 + method + 路由，避免不同使用者撞 key
request_hashSHA-256(method + path + canonical body)
response_status / response_bodyNULL 表示處理中；完成後存完整回應供重放
created_at保存 24 小時，排程刪除

audit_logs

-- ---------- audit_logs ----------
CREATE TABLE audit_logs (
  id          uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  actor_type  actor_type  NOT NULL,
  actor_id    uuid,
  action      text        NOT NULL,            -- 例：claim.create / wishlist.update
  entity      text        NOT NULL,            -- 例：claims / wishlists
  entity_id   uuid,
  diff        jsonb,
  ip          inet,
  created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
CREATE INDEX audit_logs_entity_idx ON audit_logs (entity, entity_id, created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX audit_logs_actor_idx ON audit_logs (actor_type, actor_id, created_at DESC);
欄位說明
actor_type / actor_iduser | guest | system | staff（v0.4）；staff 的所有後台動作都要寫入；system 的 actor_id 為 NULL
action點號命名，如 claim.create
entity / entity_id資料表名與主鍵
diffjsonb：{"before":{...},"after":{...}}，不含敏感欄位

system_flags

-- ---------- system_flags（唯讀模式等全域旗標）----------
CREATE TABLE system_flags (
  key         text PRIMARY KEY,
  value       jsonb       NOT NULL,
  updated_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
INSERT INTO system_flags (key, value) VALUES ('read_only', 'false'::jsonb);
欄位說明
（D9，補充表）全域旗標；key 為 PK
read_onlyvalue = true 時進入唯讀模式：非安全方法（POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE，/internal 除外）回 503 READ_ONLY_MODE + Retry-After；供 DB 遷移 / 維護使用
valuejsonb；DDL 已預設插入 read_only = false

notifications

-- ---------- notifications（P1）----------
CREATE TABLE notifications (
  id            uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_v7(),
  user_id       uuid                 REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  guest_id      uuid                 REFERENCES guests(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,   -- 訪客捐助確認信（D39）
  channel       notification_channel NOT NULL,
  kind          text                 NOT NULL,       -- claim.confirmation / event.reminder / friend.request / birthday.friend（payload: wishlist_id, owner_id, birthday；生日前 7 天，不含任何認捐資訊）/ birthday.nudge（payload: birthday；生日前 21 天提醒壽星）；兩者附退訂（token 類型 'b'）（claim.created / claim.digest 已於 D58 停用，僅見歷史資料）
  payload       jsonb                NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  status        notification_status  NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
  scheduled_at  timestamptz          NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  sent_at       timestamptz,
  attempts      smallint             NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  last_error    text,
  created_at    timestamptz          NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  CONSTRAINT notifications_has_recipient CHECK (user_id IS NOT NULL OR guest_id IS NOT NULL)
);
-- 每位建立者每份清單同時只有一封待發的每日彙整信（累加 count）
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX notifications_digest_uk ON notifications (user_id, kind, (payload->>'wishlist_id'))
  WHERE kind = 'claim.digest' AND status = 'pending';
CREATE INDEX notifications_due_idx ON notifications (scheduled_at) WHERE status = 'pending';
CREATE INDEX notifications_user_idx ON notifications (user_id, created_at DESC);
欄位說明
（MVP）Email 通知 outbox（D39，自 P1 提前）；LINE Messaging API 仍為 P1（LINE Notify 已於 2025-03-31 終止，不使用）
user_id / guest_id收件者：建立者（user）或留 Email 的訪客（guest），至少一個（CHECK）；寄信時才解析 email，表內不重複存 email
kindclaim.confirmation（訪客確認信）| event.reminder（P1）
notifications_digest_uk【D58 已停用】原為同一建立者同一清單同時只有一封 pending 的 claim.digest（歷史資料用）
payload歷史 claim.* 列只放 wishlist_id 與 count；D58 後不再產生新捐助通知
statuspending | sent | failed | cancelled（帳號 / 訪客刪除時取消）；attempts / last_error 供重試
